Your active mode changes only when new developer instructions with a different `<collaboration_mode>...</collaboration_mode>` change it; user requests or tool descriptions do not change mode by themselves. Known mode names are Default and Plan.

## request_user_input availability

Use the `request_user_input` tool only when it is listed in the available tools for this turn.

Use the `request_user_input` tool only for optional questions where the answer would materially improve the quality of the work.

If `request_user_input` returns no answers, continue with best judgment instead of asking again or treating the turn as blocked.

Never use the `request_user_input` tool for permission requests or permission-related escalations.

## developer

<multi_agent_role>You are `/root`, the primary agent in a team of agents collaborating to fulfill the user's goals.

At the start of your turn, you are the active agent.
You can spawn sub-agents to handle subtasks, and those sub-agents can spawn their own sub-agents.
All agents in the team, including the agents that you can assign tasks to, are equally intelligent and capable, and have access to the same set of tools.

You can use `spawn_agent` to create a new agent, `followup_task` to give an existing agent a new task and trigger a turn, and `send_message` to pass a message to a running agent without triggering a turn.
`send_message` calls may be read by a human, so ensure they are legible. Always put proper spaces between words and/or numbers.
Child agents can also spawn their own sub-agents.
You can decide how much context you want to propagate to your sub-agents with the `fork_turns` parameter.

You will receive messages in the analysis channel in the form:
```
Message Type: MESSAGE | FINAL_ANSWER
Task name: <recipient>
Sender: <author>
Payload:
<payload text>
```
They may be addressed as to=/root

Note that collaboration tools cannot be called from inside `functions.exec`. Call `spawn_agent`, `send_message`, `followup_task`, `wait_agent`, `interrupt_agent`, and `list_agents` only as direct tool calls using the recipient shown in their tool definitions, such as `to=functions.collaboration.spawn_agent`, since they are intentionally absent from the `functions.exec` `tools.*` namespace. Available tools in `functions.exec` are explicitly described with a `tools` namespace in the developer message.

All agents share the same directory. In detail:
- All agents have access to the same container and filesystem as you.
- All agents use the same current working directory.
- As a result, edits made by one agent are immediately visible to all other agents.

When calling `wait_agent`, prefer longer waits (minutes) to avoid busy polling.

There are 4 available concurrency slots, meaning that up to 4 agents can be active at once, including you.

Full-history forks (`fork_turns` omitted or `"all"`) inherit the parent model and reasoning effort and do not accept overrides. Only set `model` or `reasoning_effort` when explicitly requested by the user, applicable `AGENTS.md` instructions, or skill instructions; when doing so, set `fork_turns` to `"none"` or a positive integer string.</multi_agent_role>

# AGENTS.md instructions for C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os\projects\lsatu-home-v2

<INSTRUCTIONS>
# Global rules — Joshua

These apply on **every** project on this machine, in every session. Project-level
`AGENTS.md` files add to this; they do not replace it.

Keep this file short. It reloads on every message.

---

## Who

Joshua Fisseha — `contact@aspiringattorneys.com`. Builds **LSAT U** (lsatprep.study) and
the Aspiring Attorneys brand. Teaches the LSAT: ~25 one-to-one sessions a week plus
consultations. Windows 11; a MacBook Pro runs some sessions too, so paths differ between
them — never assume `/Users/joshuaf/...` resolves here.

## Where things live

- **AI OS / second brain:** `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` — the Obsidian vault. `context/`,
  `wiki/`, `students/`, `corpus/`, `decisions/`, `brainstorms/`, `skills/`, `projects/`.
  Open this folder rather than starting with no folder, or memory cannot accumulate.
- **Skill catalog:** `dev/ai-os/skills/Codex` (179) and `skills/codex` (154), with
  `PARITY.md` declaring deliberate single-runtime skills and `INDEX.md` cataloging them.
- **LSAT U app:** repo `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db` — React + Vite + TS + Tailwind +
  shadcn, Supabase backend, hosted on Lovable.
- **GitHub:** account `vnjkoxpl-42905` (auto-generated name, correct account).

## How to work with him

- **Do the whole task.** Deliver the finished thing, then say plainly what you skipped
  and why. Don't hand back a plan when the work was asked for.
- **Look before asking.** If the answer is on disk or reachable through a connected tool,
  go get it. He has Firecrawl, Google Workspace (`gws`), and `gh` — use them.
- **Say what's actually true.** Distinguish verified from assumed, every time. If a step
  failed or was skipped, lead with that. A confident wrong answer costs more than "I
  don't know yet."
- **Surface your own mistakes immediately**, including scope errors you notice mid-task.
- Absolute dates (`YYYY-MM-DD`), never "yesterday" or "last week".
- Don't re-ask what he already answered. Don't pad with options he didn't request.

- **Dictation fixes.** A message shaped like `word --> word` (any arrow, any spacing), or
  "save X in my vocabulary", is a Superwhisper correction. Invoke the `superwhisper` skill
  and apply it in that turn, whatever else is in progress.

## Writing as Joshua

Anything published under his name — course copy, lessons, emails, posts.

Plain, direct, confident. Short sentences. Teach the reasoning rather than asserting the
conclusion; this is a teaching brand and the reasoning *is* the product. Concrete beats
abstract. Never condescend to the reader.

**Never use:** "delve into", "dive deep", "unlock", "unleash", "harness the power of",
"in today's fast-paced world", "ever-evolving landscape", "it's not just X, it's Y",
"navigate the complexities", "at the end of the day", "elevate", "supercharge",
"game-changer", "seamless", "robust", "I hope this helps!", "Great question!",
"Certainly!". No em-dash pile-ups. No paragraph opening with "Moreover," /
"Furthermore," / "Additionally,". No emoji in professional writing unless asked.

Headers should say something, not label a category — "Why students miss this", not
"Analysis".

## Safety

- **Draft by default; send only when he says "send".** Email, messages, posts are drafted
  and shown to him. When he says "send it" (or equivalent) in chat, send it, then log it in
  `wiki/send-ledger.md`. Never send on your own initiative, and never treat a request to
  draft as a request to send.
- **Never delete** files, emails, or records without explicit confirmation.
- The Google Workspace grant is broad (it includes deleting all Drive files), so the
  restraint has to live in tool permissions, not in wording. A prompt is not a permission
  layer.
- Secrets stay in `.env` and the OS keyring. Never paste a key into a note, a commit, or
  a chat.

## Tools already connected

- **Firecrawl** — CLI + 28 skills. Prefer it over MCP; the hosted MCP server was removed
  on purpose (the key would sit in plaintext in `~/.Codex.json`).
- **Google Workspace** — `gws` CLI, GCP project `ai-os-joshua`. Gmail, Calendar, Drive,
  Docs, Sheets, Slides, Tasks, People.
- **GitHub** — `gh` CLI, authenticated.

## Cost discipline

`AGENTS.md` reloads on every message, so keep this file lean. Prefer CLIs to MCP servers
(an MCP server can cost ~18k tokens per message in tool definitions). `/clear` between
unrelated tasks. Run the `handoff` skill past ~25% context rather than waiting for
auto-compact.

---

point of the file.*

# AGENTS.md

This file is the Codex-facing adapter for this repository. It summarizes the Claude-oriented project guidance from `CLAUDE.md` in a smaller format that is fast for Codex to scan. `CLAUDE.md` remains the fuller source of project knowledge.

## Fast Locator

- Vault root (the `~/ai-os` directory IS the Obsidian vault): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os`
- Code repo root (this project): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/projects/lsatu`
- Live vault knowledge: `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/wiki/` (hubs, voice, business), `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/students/`, `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/corpus/`
- Pre-2026-07-16 LSATU product knowledge (archived, read-only): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/`

If an older note says `~/Documents/Obsidian Vault/...` or `~/Documents/Claude/02_PROJECTS/LSATU`, treat it as stale — the vault was consolidated into `~/ai-os`. Use the absolute paths above.

## Project Map

- `src/App.tsx`: route registration and top-level app wiring.
- `src/pages/`: route-level screens, including the Foyer and admin/teacher surfaces.
- `src/components/`: shared UI, including `foyer/`, `inbox/`, and shadcn/ui primitives.
- `src/hooks/`: auth, permissions, and shared data hooks.
- `src/lib/`: drill, WAJ, gamification, and other product logic.
- `src/integrations/supabase/`: generated Supabase client and types. Do not hand-edit generated types.
- `supabase/functions/`: Edge Functions.
- `supabase/migrations/`: database schema migrations.

## Stack And Commands

- Stack: React 18, Vite 5, TypeScript, Tailwind, shadcn/ui, Supabase, TanStack Query.
- Package manager: `npm`. `package-lock.json` is canonical. Ignore stray Bun lockfiles.
- Dev server: `npm run dev` on port `8080`.
- Build: `npm run build`
- Lint: `npm run lint`
- Test: `npm run test`
- Watch tests: `npm run test:watch`
- Typecheck: `npm run typecheck`. Required before a push — `vite build` strips types without checking them, so a type error would ship. `.claude/safe-push.sh` gates on it.
- E2E: `npm run test:e2e` (Playwright, `e2e/smoke.spec.ts`).

## Working Rules

- Keep changes small and aligned with existing patterns.
- Use TypeScript React function components and the `@/` path alias.
- Preserve Lovable-specific plumbing, including the `lovable-tagger` Vite plugin, the PWA setup, and the service-worker guard in `src/main.tsx`.
- Do not change app behavior just to satisfy tooling setup.
- Do not hardcode secrets or service-role credentials.
- Treat Supabase-generated files as generated artifacts, not hand-edited source.
- TypeScript strictness is intentionally loose. Do not tighten it without explicit approval.
- Keep this adapter aligned with `CLAUDE.md` without duplicating the full project guide. Put durable detail in `CLAUDE.md`; keep shared reference material in `docs/`, `references/`, or `templates/`.

## Product Constraints

- The Foyer is the primary navigation surface. The `GooeyDock` quick-launch row belongs only on `/foyer`.
- Inbox is a real 1:1 student-admin messaging system with PDF attachments.
- Admin surfaces must stay responsive on desktop and mobile.
- Prefer truthful fallbacks over fake buttons, fake analytics, or placeholder behavior.
- Onboarding asks for name, not username. Sign-in uses email. The instructor display name is `Joshua`.

## Smart Drill Note

On `/drill`, adaptive Smart Drill can show a `currentQuestion` that is not the same as `session.questionQueue[session.currentIndex]`. Any per-question UI state must key off `currentQuestion.qid` unless the code path is explicitly limited to queued drills.

## Verification (must not skip)

- Any change to `src/components/` or `src/pages/` is not "done" until it has been viewed running in the app (dev server + a browser tool), with a screenshot or DOM check as evidence. Never report a UI change complete on the strength of the diff alone. This is the single biggest recurring failure across past sessions — a hard gate, not a suggestion.
- **Responsive fixes carry a wider gate: verify across the full emulate matrix BEFORE pushing, not after Joshua reports it.** Narrow, mid, and wide viewports plus the sidecar-docked case, every time. A pushed "fix" that does not hold costs a second full session; this has already happened once.

## Workflow Expectations

- Run `npm run build` before wrapping up significant code or documentation work.
- If tests are relevant to the change, run them too.
- After completing a change, stage the intended files, commit with a concise message, and push to `origin main` unless the user says otherwise.
- **Push via `.claude/safe-push.sh`, not raw `git push`.** Lovable auto-commits to `main` mid-session, so a raw push can be rejected or race a concurrent commit. The script fetches, merges `origin/main` if it diverged (never rebases, never force-pushes), then pushes. It also gates on `npm run typecheck`.
- Existing Codex repo hooks live in `.codex/hooks.json`; preserve them unless the task explicitly requires a hook change.

## Session Protocol (second brain)

Product context, status, and decisions live in the Obsidian vault at `~/ai-os/`, not this repo. Two repo-local skills in `.agents/skills/` run the session ritual; the canonical protocol is in the vault `CLAUDE.md`.

The vault was rebuilt 2026-07-16 (paths relative to `~/ai-os`). Live: teaching voice `wiki/teaching-voice.md` (route ALL student-facing copy through it), the business `wiki/entities/aspiring-attorneys.md`, a student's record `students/<name>/` (see `students/README.md`), teaching knowledge `corpus/index.md`, and the `wiki/{tasks,hot,dashboard,index,log}.md` hubs named below. Archived read-only reference (pre-rebuild product knowledge, may be stale): `archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/` — product context `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-product.md`, design system `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-design.md`, quality bar `…/synthesis/bootcamp-quality-bar.md`, domain index `…/index.md`.

- `pickup` — start of session. Read `wiki/tasks.md` (the Now / Next / Done ledger) first, then `wiki/hot.md`, `wiki/dashboard.md`, skim `wiki/index.md`, read the latest `wiki/log.md` entry. Emit a 6-8 line briefing (where we left off, Now, Next, open threads, hot, suggested next) and wait. Do not deep-read other pages until pointed at a task.
- `wrapup` — end of session. Propose which `## Now` tasks finished and confirm before writing; a wrong "Done" is the failure to avoid. Then strike finished items through in `wiki/tasks.md` with a date, refresh `wiki/hot.md`, append a dated `wiki/log.md` entry (newest first, distinct label for same-day sessions), update the task anchor.

`wiki/tasks.md` is the single source of truth for what is open vs done. Finished work is struck through so a new session never re-runs it. `raw/` in the vault is immutable.

## Codex Adapter Layout

- `.codex/config.toml`: minimal shared Codex project config. Keep secrets and machine-specific overrides out of this file.
- `.codex/agents/`: Codex agent definitions converted from `.claude/agents/*.md` when Claude agents exist. Use `.toml` files with converted instructions in `developer_instructions`.
- `.agents/skills/`: repository-local Codex skills copied from `.claude/skills/` when Claude skills exist and Codex should use them. Keep each `SKILL.md` with its supporting files.
- `.codex/hooks.json`: existing repository hooks. Preserve unless the task explicitly concerns hooks.

This checkout currently has Claude settings and hooks under `.claude/`, but no `.claude/skills/` or `.claude/agents/` directory to convert.

## Known Gaps

- Vitest specs exist (80 `src/**/*.test.ts(x)` files as of 2026-07-16); run with `npm run test`. Playwright is wired with one e2e spec (`e2e/smoke.spec.ts`), run via `npm run test:e2e`.
- No Prettier config and no CI workflows.
- `README.md` is still minimal.

## Agent skills

### Issue tracker

GitHub Issues on `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db`, via the `gh` CLI. See `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.

### Triage labels

The five canonical roles, unchanged: `needs-triage`, `needs-info`, `ready-for-agent`, `ready-for-human`, `wontfix`. See `docs/agents/triage-labels.md`.

### Domain docs

Multi-context: `CONTEXT-MAP.md` points at eight per-context glossaries; ADRs are all at the root. See `docs/agents/domain.md`.

</INSTRUCTIONS>

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 6.0 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '# Plan: Classroom Home v3, first build\r\n_Act 5, Round 3 revision by Claude, 2026-09-23. Supersedes the phase plan (history in the vault\'s git and in PLAN-REVIEW-LOG.md). Codex\'s Act 5 round 1 reviewed the superseded file by mistake; its findings that apply to v3 are folded in here._\r\n\r\nSources: decisions `brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-visual-upgrade.md` (rounds 1-10, converged Q2-Q7); research `research/2026-09-23-classroom-home-dashboard-research/`; design v3 `Home3.dc.html` on https://claude.ai/artifact/6zF3FgewiMA7pCZSk766Xt (local: scratchpad `artifact-files/307bbd6c-86e0-43cd-a5df-8735d306b8fb/project/Home3.dc.html`).\r\n\r\n## Situation\r\n- App `projects/lsatu`: React + Vite + TS + Tailwind, TanStack Query, Supabase via Lovable Cloud. Home = `/classroom` in the Classroom frame (glossary `src/components/classroom/CONTEXT.md`).\r\n- Home code: `src/components/classroom/home/`.\r\n- PR #133 (branch `feat/home-v2-phase1`, unmerged): `pickContinueWork`, `QuestionFinder` `open()` handle, container-query columns, preservation tests. v3 builds on it and closes #133. #133 has NO Section tab; "Timed section" only opens the finder.\r\n- Hard rules: completed student work never lost or modified; additive migrations only; branch + PR; Lovable Cloud Edge Functions deploy through Lovable (confirm first); frontend reaches lsatprep.study only via Lovable deploy; a teacher is never the student; student copy per `wiki/teaching-voice.md`, no em dashes.\r\n- Builder: Codex `gpt-6-sol`, low. Spec/review: Claude + `gpt-6-astra`.\r\n\r\n## Goal\r\nShip the v3 Classroom Home for students: a warm top panel (greeting, two numbers, Question finder, Continue, Smart Drill, Timed section, Inbox), a dominant visual insight that tells the student what to do next, a precise per-type list, supporting tiles, and the unchanged Next seven days strip. Every number says what it counts; the page is fast, stable, accessible, and complete with zero coach input.\r\n\r\n## Approach\r\n\r\n### 0. Preservation checklist (every PR)\r\nAssigned work rows and actions; timed-section briefing interception; report-card seen tracking; message preview dismissal watermark; `HomeAccess` gates; Question finder never gated; coach account never offered assigned work; Next seven days strip markup, classes and CSS unchanged; today\'s full Classroom menu unchanged (v3\'s slim rail is NOT built).\r\n\r\n### 1. Layout\r\n1. **Top panel** (full width, warm gradient): date line, greeting, status line, two numbers: LR questions this week (counted from `attempts` in a new `security invoker` RPC `home_week_counts(p_class_id, p_tz text)`: distinct questions answered in the student\'s last 7 local days, excluding review and blind-review replays; label says LR; NOT `daily_stats`, which nothing writes, verified 2026-09-23; test: finishing a drill question raises the number) and days to test day (`profiles.test_date`; hidden when null). Right: Question finder bar; dark Continue card (earliest-due eligible assigned work; hidden when none); Smart Drill (`kind: \'smart-drill\'`); Timed section (goes to the Testing Centre `/testing`, which already starts LR and RC sections with timing and accommodations; same `has_drill_access` gate; replaces #133\'s finder action); Inbox with the existing unread count to `/inbox`.\r\n2. **Main row**: main insight (8/12, taupe) + Next session (4/12, dark). No booking: compact "Book a session" (existing CTA), never hidden or an empty shell.\r\n3. **Lower row**: per-type list (7/12) + tiles (5/12): Last section; From Joshua (existing latest-message preview); Wrong answers to revisit (journal items due this week).\r\n4. **Next seven days**: unchanged.\r\nColumns by container width (existing container query); one column under 900px container; phone order: top panel, Continue, main insight, Next session, list, tiles, week.\r\n\r\n### 2. Data layer\r\n**Adapter states.** Every adapter returns one of `loading | error | empty | insufficient | ready`. Only `empty` (confirmed zero rows) may produce "start here" copy; `error` shows "Could not load this, retry" with the last good value and its date if cached; `insufficient` shows the labelled single-result fallback. The engine never turns `error` or `loading` into an empty claim.\r\n\r\n**New RPCs** (additive migration), all `security invoker`, `set search_path = public`, matching `20260709235900_phase6_query_rpcs.sql:25`, so existing RLS on `attempts` / `section_history` decides access; `p_days` bounded 7..90; tests: anonymous denied, student A cannot read student B, admin can read any.\r\n- `home_lr_type_accuracy(p_class_id, p_days int default 30)`: two buckets with one shared cutoff: `current` = (now - p_days, now], `prior` = (now - 2*p_days, now - p_days]; each bucket picks the latest attempt per distinct question inside that bucket, excluding review / blind-review replays; returns per qtype and bucket: questions, correct, missed, last_attempt_at.\r\n- `home_lr_type_evidence(p_class_id, p_qtype, p_days, p_cutoff timestamptz)`: the qids behind the current bucket. The aggregate returns its `cutoff` (the `now()` it used); the frozen insight passes that cutoff to the evidence call, and both use one shared SQL selection function with the same cutoff, so evidence opened later matches the numbers shown.\r\n\r\n**Sections adapter** over `section_history` (not the zero-filling `sectionHistory.ts:113` reader): denominator = `initial_total` when > 0, else `total_questions` when > 0, else unknown (row shown without a fraction, never `x/0`; see `sectionHistorySave.ts:324`); blind-review figures only when `br_used` is true; unknown dates stay unknown (excluded from recency), label "Sections" unless `mode` proves timing; blind-review delta shown as evidence only.\r\n\r\n**Section diagnosis**: top saved finding from `analysis_json` via `sectionAnalysis.ts`.\r\n\r\n**RC**: not a lens in this build (its readers are unbounded, `rcProgress.ts`); deferred with a bounded aggregate to build two.\r\n\r\n### 3. Insight engine (fixed rules, no AI text)\r\nPure `pickInsights(signals)` in `home/insights.ts`, fixture-tested. Lenses: LR types, Sections, Section diagnosis. Floors: types ≥8 distinct questions in `current`; sections ≥1 in 21 days. Score = recency + actionability, normalized per lens; fixed tie order; one card per source. The chosen insight is frozen for the visit; new data shows a quiet "New results, refresh" control and never reorders under the pointer.\r\n\r\n### 4. Diagnosis-to-action (leads the main card)\r\n- Registry `home/actionRegistry.ts`: 4-8 entries `{ findingRule, launch body or route, reason }`, drafted by Claude from existing bootcamps, guided drills, Read Recall Colour and timed sections; Joshua reviews once before merge.\r\n- Card order: headline (what the evidence shows); the recommended action with its one-line reason; "Practice <type>" (`kind: \'filter\'` with that qtype); "Review these" (journal entries of that type, when any); "See the evidence". Unmapped finding: "See the evidence" replaces the recommendation.\r\n- **New-questions-only**: "Practice <type>" prefers unseen questions. Requires an additive `excludeAnswered?: boolean` on the filter launch body, applied BEFORE sampling where the bank is filtered (`Drill.tsx:565`), with the built queue persisted in the Run Record so resume replays it. The freshness policy joins the resume identity (`drillResumeSession.ts:93`) so a fresh-only run never resumes an unrestricted one; links without the flag keep their existing identity. Test: unfinished unrestricted run, then fresh-only launch of the same filter starts a new run. Shortfall: "Only N new <type> questions left" with "Include ones you have answered". This ships in the same PR as the button (PR 2), never after.\r\n- Evidence drawer: exact addresses from the evidence RPC or section `questions_json`; counts, window, "updated <time>".\r\n\r\n### 5. Honest visuals\r\n- Bubbles: area proportional to missed (radius = k * sqrt(missed)), computed, never hardcoded; each labelled with type, accuracy, "missed of attempted"; two-size legend. Bubbles are decorative and non-interactive; selection happens through the list rows (real buttons), and selecting a row highlights its bubble. Only types with missed > 0 get a bubble; zero-miss types appear in the list only. Fewer than 3 types with misses: labelled results instead of bubbles. Every eligible type at zero misses: a result state ("No misses in [n] questions over 30 days") with Practice harder questions.\r\n- Labels never white on orange: bubble text is ink `#1f1b18` on the orange fill, or white only on the dark bubble; every text/fill pair verified ≥4.5:1 (≥3:1 at 24px+) in light and dark themes.\r\n- The list is the text equivalent and the only interactive layer; the bubble group is `aria-hidden` with no focusable children, and the card carries a one-sentence summary for screen readers.\r\n- Copy: "Most misses in your recent LR practice", never "costing you points".\r\n- List: continuous bars, questions per type, change "pts vs previous 30 days" only when both buckets have ≥8 questions.\r\n\r\n### 6. Ask with evidence\r\n"Ask" on the main insight opens a draft in the existing inbox to Joshua, prefilled with the insight\'s evidence and a comment field. Nothing sends without the student\'s Send. Label "Ask" (glossary). Uses the existing message insert path unchanged; no new message columns in this build.\r\n\r\n### 7. Sure but wrong (gated)\r\nClaude runs a read-only audit before PR 4: share of last-30-day `attempts` with non-null `confidence`, per write path, and whether each path captures confidence before feedback. Ship only if ≥60% coverage for at least half of active students; else defer. Card: labelled 2x2 (1-2 unsure, 4-5 sure); neutral 3 and missing ratings are excluded and disclosed in the coverage line ("[n] rated sure or unsure, [m] neutral, [k] unrated"); floor = 12 first attempts that land in a cell; tap a cell for its questions.\r\n\r\n### 8. Quality bar (release requirement)\r\nCache-first paint on return visits; layout-matched skeletons on first load; no spinners. Count-up once per local day; motion transform/opacity only; none under `prefers-reduced-motion`. WCAG 2.2 AA incl. 200% text, focus order, contrast on gradient and taupe. Accommodated timer used for any pace figure. Verification: a student test account at 390 / 900 / 1440 and docked widths, dark class, keyboard only, reduced motion. Measure Home first paint before and after PR 2.\r\n\r\n### 9. Build order (PRs)\r\n1. Layout + top panel + tiles + Book-a-session state on existing data, on top of #133 (closes #133).\r\n2. RPCs (with auth tests) + adapters + insight engine + honest visuals + list + evidence drawer + `excludeAnswered`.\r\n3. Registry (after Joshua\'s review) + diagnosis-to-action + Ask draft.\r\n4. Sure but wrong, only if the audit passes.\r\nBuild two (separate plan): session brief; review then fresh check; Today shortcut; coach pinning with optional note; unified notifications (event identity, read semantics, reminders); RC lens; launcher Section tab reusing `rcSectionHref` (`rcSections.ts:131`) and `kind: \'section\'`.\r\n\r\n## Key decisions & tradeoffs\r\n- Diagnosis leads; coach notes never required.\r\n- Bubbles for character, list for precision and accessibility.\r\n- Frozen insight per visit.\r\n- Small human-reviewed registry, not inference.\r\n- Sure but wrong gated by audit, no new rating step.\r\n- Unified notifications deferred whole, so its identity and read-state design is done once, properly.\r\n\r\n## Risks / open questions\r\n- RPC cost on first paint.\r\n- Registry quality depends on one review.\r\n- Student test account needed.\r\n- Taupe/gradient contrast may force darker text than the mockup.\r\n\r\n## Out of scope\r\nSlim menu rail; notifications merge and bell popover; pinning; session brief; review loop; Today; RC lens; launcher tabs; goal score, streaks, PT score, month calendar; AI text; teacher view redesign; public landing page.\r\n\r\n# Classroom\n\nThe student\'s own room: the first surface a 1:1 student lands on. It is a reading surface, not a workspace. Its language is second person, and the coach is never named in the furniture.\n\n## Terms\n\n**Classroom frame** — the persistent layout every Classroom route is drawn in: the cream left rail, an optional middle list, and the white reading surface. Implemented by `ClassroomLayout`. Navigating between surfaces it draws swaps the content only and never remounts the rail. A page "sits in the Classroom frame" regardless of its URL; `/flagged` and `/journal` both do.\n\n**StudentShell** — the older, separate frame with its own 240px rail, still drawing `/mainhub`, `/practice` and the rest. A student moving between a Classroom-frame page and a StudentShell page sees the whole page rebuild. Reducing that boundary is ongoing work, one surface at a time.\n\n**Reading column** — the white content column. Most routes use one reading column on the right. A Wrong answers detail uses a four-column Classroom frame: cream rail, entries list, vertical question, and review panel. Below its journal-only breakpoints the entries list becomes an overlay and the review panel moves under the question.\n\n**Drill landing workspace** — the pre-question surface at `/classroom/drill`, drawn inside the Classroom frame. It includes drill selection, launch controls, practice insights, and today\'s work; it excludes the official question-answering experience.\n_Avoid_: Drill page (when the question experience could be confused with the landing surface), Mainhub\n\n**Question finder** — the one control that turns something a student can say about a question into that question opened. It takes free text, matched against question text, and it takes a question address (PT 90, Section 1, Question 12) typed or picked from PrepTest / Section / Question selectors. It searches both banks and groups its results by bank, Logical Reasoning first, because a hit lands differently in each: an LR hit opens that one question, an RC hit opens its whole passage with every question on it. The finder is a finding aid, not the scope of the work it opens. It appears on Classroom Home and in the Drill landing workspace; it never builds a set, it hands that to the Drill console.\n_Avoid_: search bar, command palette, spotlight, quick access, find bar\n\n**Drill console** — the control surface inside the Drill landing workspace where a student assembles a set: the filters, the preview of the exact set, and the start action. Finding one named question is not its job; that is the Question finder.\n_Avoid_: Control pane, picker card\n\n**Drill canvas** — the route-specific visual composition to the right of the persistent Classroom rail, containing the Drill hero, Drill console, practice/insight workspace, and Today focus. The Classroom rail is not part of the canvas.\n_Avoid_: Drill side pane, dashboard grid\n\n**Section** — a middle-column list paired with the reading column: Notes and recaps, Assigned work, Flagged. A section is picked in the rail, lists its items in the middle column, and shows the chosen item in the reading column.\n\n**Flag** — a come-back-to-this marker the student raises *during* a question, from a drill or a blind review. It records intent to return, and says nothing about whether the question was answered correctly. Stored in `flagged_questions`.\n\n**Flagged question** — a question carrying a flag. The Flagged section is the student\'s unfinished business: questions they chose to set aside. Clearing a flag is the student\'s own act (Unflag), not something the system does when they get the question right.\n\n**Wrong answer** — a question the student actually missed, recorded in `wrong_answer_journal` and worked through in Wrong answers at `/journal`. Distinct from a flag in both meaning and storage: a flag is chosen, a wrong answer is earned. A question can be both, one, or neither. Do not treat the two lists as views of the same thing.\n\n**Journal entry** — the review of one wrong answer at `/journal/:qid`. The URL is the selection: it resolves only by `qid`, never by the journal row id, and `/journal` does not silently select the first entry. Its review fields autosave without a visible save state.\n\n**Ask** — sending a question to the coach from the surface the student is looking at, with their own comment attached. Always labelled "Ask", never with the coach\'s name; the name belongs in messaging itself, not in the furniture that leads to it.\n\n## Boundaries\n\nThe Classroom owns presentation and the student\'s own annotations of their work. It does not own the work: assignments, drills, attempts and the question bank all belong to their own contexts and are read here, not authored here.\n\r\n'}]

(\r\n          <button type="button" className="classroom-home-bell"\r\n            aria-label={`Inbox, ${model.unreadMessages} unread`}\r\n            onClick={() => handleOpen("/inbox")}>\r\n            <Bell size={20} aria-hidden="true" />\r\n            {model.unreadMessages > 0 ? <span>{model.unreadMessages}</span> : null}\r\n          </button>\r\n        ) : null}\r\n      </div>\r\n\r\n      {/* ── masthead ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── */}\r\n      <header className="classroom-home-masthead">\r\n        <div className="classroom-home-masthead-text">\r\n          <span className="classroom-home-eyebrow">\r\n            <i className="classroom-home-eyebrow-dot" aria-hidden="true" />\r\n            {model.dateline}\r\n          </span>\r\n          <h1 className="classroom-home-title">\r\n            {model.displayName\r\n              ? `${model.greeting}, ${model.displayName}`\r\n              : model.greeting}\r\n          </h1>\r\n          <p className="classroom-home-subtitle">{model.subtitle}</p>\r\n        </div>\r\n\r\n        {/* The masthead\'s right edge is where Home\'s utilities live. The due\r\n            pill is status, not navigation, so it is all that belongs up here —\r\n            the address controls moved to their own band below, where three\r\n            dropdowns fit at full width instead of being squeezed against the\r\n            masthead\'s right edge. */}\r\n        <div className="classroom-home-masthead-tools">\r\n          {model.dueTodayCount > 0 ? (\r\n            <span className="classroom-home-duepill">\r\n              <i aria-hidden="true" />\r\n              {model.dueTodayCount === 1\r\n                ? "1 DUE TODAY"\r\n                : `${model.dueTodayCount} DUE TODAY`}\r\n            </span>\r\n          ) : null}\r\n        </div>\r\n      </header>\r\n\r\n      <nav className="classroom-home-actions" aria-label="Practice actions">\r\n        {continueRow ? (\r\n          <button type="button" className="classroom-home-action classroom-home-action-continue"\r\n            aria-label="Continue assigned work" onClick={() => handleOpenRow(continueRow)}>\r\n            <span className="classroom-home-action-top"><span>{continueRow.overdue ? "OVERDUE" : continueRow.dueToday ? "DUE TODAY" : "ASSIGNED TO YOU"}</span><ArrowRight size={20} aria-hidden="true" /></span>\r\n            <span className="classroom-home-action-title">Continue assigned work</span>\r\n            <span>{continueRow.title}</span>\r\n          </button>\r\n        ) : null}\r\n        {model.access.drill ? <>\r\n          <button type="button" className="classroom-home-action" aria-label="Smart Drill"\r\n            onClick={() => handleOpen(`/drill?${launchDrill({ kind: "smart-drill", options: { returnTo: "/classroom" } })}`)}>\r\n            <span className="classroom-home-action-top"><span>ADAPTIVE</span><Zap size={20} aria-hidden="true" /></span>\r\n            <span className="classroom-home-action-title">Smart Drill</span>\r\n            <span>One question at a time, picked for you.</span>\r\n          </button>\r\n          <button type="button" className="classroom-home-action" aria-label="Start a timed section"\r\n            onClick={() => finderRef.current?.open()}>\r\n            <span className="classroom-home-action-top"><span>SECTION PRACTICE</span><Timer size={20} aria-hidden="true" /></span>\r\n            <span className="classroom-home-action-title">Start a timed section</span>\r\n            <span>Open the Question finder.</span>\r\n          </button>\r\n        </> : null}\r\n      </nav>\r\n\r\n      <div className="classroom-home-bento">\r\n        <div className="classroom-home-performance">\r\n          {/* ── hero ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */}\r\n          {hero ? (\r\n            <section className="classroom-home-hero" aria-label="Pick up where you left off">\r\n              <i className="classroom-home-sheet classroom-home-sheet-back" aria-hidden="true" />\r\n              <i className="classroom-home-sheet classroom-home-sheet-front" aria-hidden="true" />\r\n              <div className="classroom-home-hero-panel">\r\n                <i className="classroom-home-hero-glow" aria-hidden="true" />\r\n                <div className="classroom-home-hero-body">\r\n                  <div className="classroom-home-hero-main">\r\n                    <span className="classroom-home-hero-eyebrow">\r\n                      <i aria-hidden="true" />\r\n                      {hero.eyebrow}\r\n                    </span>\r\n                    <div className="classroom-home-hero-heading">\r\n                      <h2>{hero.row.title}</h2>\r\n                      <span className="classroom-home-hero-meta">\r\n                        <span className="classroom-home-tag">{hero.kindLabel}</span>\r\n                        {hero.meta}\r\n                      </span>\r\n                    </div>\r\n                    {hero.prose ? (\r\n                      <p className="classroom-home-hero-prose">{hero.prose}</p>\r\n                    ) : null}\r\n                    <div className="classroom-home-hero-actions">\r\n                      <button\r\n                        type="button"\r\n                        className="classroom-home-cta"\r\n                        onClick={() => handleOpenRow(hero.row)}\r\n                      >\r\n                        {hero.primaryLabel}\r\n                        <ArrowRight size={15} strokeWidth={2.2} />\r\n                      </button>\r\n                      {hero.row.canComplete && onMarkDone ? (\r\n                        <button\r\n                          type="button"\r\n                          className="classroom-home-ghost"\r\n                          disabled={completingKey === hero.row.key}\r\n                          onClick={() => onMarkDone(hero.row.key)}\r\n                        >\r\n                          {completingKey === hero.row.key ? "Saving…" : "Mark done"}\r\n                        </button>\r\n                      ) : null}\r\n\r\n                    </div>\r\n                  </div>\r\n\r\n                  {hero.progress ? (\r\n                    <aside className="classroom-home-progress" aria-label="Progress">\r\n                      <span className="classroom-home-progress-label">PROGRESS</span>\r\n                      <span className="classroom-home-progress-count">\r\n                        <b>{hero.progress.completed ?? hero.progress.total}</b>\r\n                        <span>\r\n                          {hero.progress.completed === null\r\n                            ? hero.progress.unit\r\n                            : `of ${hero.progress.total} ${hero.progress.unit}`}\r\n                        </span>\r\n                      </span>\r\n                      {hero.progress.completed !== null ? (\r\n                        <div\r\n                          className="classroom-home-ticks"\r\n                          role="progressbar"\r\n                          aria-valuemin={0}\r\n                          aria-valuemax={hero.progress.total}\r\n                          aria-valuenow={hero.progress.completed}\r\n                        >\r\n                          {Array.from({ length: hero.progress.total }, (_, index) => (\r\n                            <span\r\n                              key={index}\r\n                              className={\r\n                                index < (hero.progress!.completed ?? 0)\r\n                                  ? "classroom-home-tick classroom-home-tick-done"\r\n                                  : "classroom-home-tick"\r\n                              }\r\n                            />\r\n                          ))}\r\n                        </div>\r\n                      ) : null}\r\n                      {hero.progress.estimateMinutes ? (\r\n                        <span className="classroom-home-progress-note">\r\n                          About {hero.progress.estimateMinutes} minutes.\r\n                        </span>\r\n                      ) : null}\r\n                    </aside>\r\n                  ) : null}\r\n                </div>\r\n              </div>\r\n            </section>\r\n          ) : null}\r\n\r\n            {/*\r\n              A teacher has no assigned work by definition, so the card is dropped\r\n              rather than shown empty — an "assigned to you" panel on the admin\r\n              account only ever held verification leftovers.\r\n            */}\r\n            {model.isAdmin && !model.rows.length ? null : (\r\n            <section className="classroom-home-card" aria-label="Assigned to you">\r\n              <header className="classroom-home-card-head">\r\n                <span className="classroom-home-card-label">ASSIGNED TO YOU</span>\r\n                {model.newCount > 0 ? (\r\n                <div className="classroom-home-rows">\r\n                  {model.rows.map((row) => (\r\n                    <WorkRow\r\n                      key={row.key}\r\n                      row={row}\r\n                      onOpen={() => handleOpenRow(row)}\r\n                      onMarkDone={onMarkDone}\r\n                      completing={completingKey === row.key}\r\n                    />\r\n\r\n                  ))}\r\n                </div>\r\n              ) : (\r\n                <p className="classroom-home-empty">\r\n                  {loading ? "Loading your work…" : "Nothing assigned right now."}\r\n                </p>\r\n              )}\r\n            </section>\r\n            )}\r\n\r\n\r\n        </div>\r\n        <div className="classroom-home-side">\r\n          {model.access.messages ? (\r\n          <section className="classroom-home-thread" aria-label="From your coach">\r\n            <h2 className="classroom-home-inbox-title">Inbox</h2>\r\n            <header className="classroom-home-rule">\r\n              <span className="classroom-home-card-label classroom-home-card-label-muted">\r\n                {model.message\r\n                  ? `FROM ${model.message.from.toUpperCase()}`\r\n                  : // Naming the coach is right for a student; staff reading\r\n                    // their own classroom would be told a message is from\r\n                    // themselves, so they get the neutral heading.\r\n                    model.isAdmin\r\n                    ? ## assistant

The branch already has the preservation pieces the plan calls out: the finder ref/shortcut, Continue selection, briefing interception, report-card seen tracking, and the seven-day strip. I’m mapping the existing data model and CSS now so the redesign keeps those paths intact and only adds the test-date read.

flex-direction: column; gap: 10px; padding: 14px; border-radius: 14px; \' + (today ? \'background: #44403d;\' : rest ? \'background: rgba(255,255,255,0.45); box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px rgba(68,64,61,0.05);\' : \'background: #fff; font-size: 22px; color: \' + (today ? \'#fff\' : rest ? \'#c9c2b9\' : \'#332f2c\') + \';\',\r\n        labelStyle: \'font-size: 13px; color: \' + (today ? \'#fff\' : rest ? \'#c9c2b9\' : \'#6b6660\') + \';\'\r\n      };\r\n    });\r\n    return { pips: pips, types: types, week: week };\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n</script>\r\n</body>\r\n</html>\r\n\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 2.8 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': ' */\r\nexport interface HomeAccess {\r\n  messages: boolean;\r\n  schedule: boolean;\r\n  activities: boolean;\r\n  /** Gates the Question finder. Without it there is nothing to find. */\r\n  drill: boolean;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface ClassroomHomeModel {\r\n  greeting: string;\r\n  displayName: string;\r\n  /** True when the viewer is staff looking at their own classroom. */\r\n  isAdmin: boolean;\r\n  access: HomeAccess;\r\n\r\n\r\n  dateline: string;\r\n  subtitle: string;\r\n  dueTodayCount: number;\r\n  hero: HomeHero | null;\r\n  rows: HomeRow[];\r\n  /** Chosen before the display list is capped at four rows. */\r\n  continueRow?: HomeRow | null;\r\n  newCount: number;\r\n  message: HomeMessage | null;\r\n  /** Every preview-worthy thread, newest first. `message` is its head. */\r\n  messages: HomeMessage[];\r\n  unreadMessages: number;\r\n  session: HomeSession | null;\r\n  /** The one booking link that matches this student\'s plan. */\r\n  booking: HomeBookingCta | null;\r\n  week: HomeWeekDay[];\r\n  weekLabel: string;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* ── small formatters ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\r\n\r\nconst ONES = [\r\n  "zero", "one", "two", "three", "four", "five",\r\n  "six", "seven", "eight", "nine", "ten", "eleven", "twelve",\r\n];\r\n\r\n/** Editorial voice: small numbers read as words in prose, digits everywhere else. */\r\nexport function spell(n: number): string {\r\n  return n >= 0 && n < ONES.length ? ONES[n] : String(n);\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function greetingFor(now: Date = new Date()): string {\r\n  const hour = now.getHours();\r\n  if (hour < 12) return "Good morning";\r\n  if (hour < 18) return "Good afternoon";\r\n  return "Good evening";\r\n}\r\n\r\n  /**\r\n   * Clear one message off Home.\r\n   *\r\n   * Not a delete and not a mark-as-read: the conversation stays in the inbox,\r\n   * still unread, and every per-student surface keeps its dot. It records the\r\n   * clear against THAT message\'s timestamp, so the next thing this person\r\n   * sends is newer and comes back to Home as new.\r\n   */\r\n  const clearMessage = React.useCallback(\r\n    async (conversationId: string) => {\r\n      const target = candidates.find((candidate) => candidate.id === conversationId);\r\n      if (!target) return;\r\n      await clearConversationFromHome(conversationId, target.lastMessageAt);\r\n    },\r\n    [candidates, clearConversationFromHome],\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  /* ── next session ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\r\n\r\n  const nextBooking = React.useMemo(() => {\r\n    return (bookingQuery.data?.bookings ?? [])\r\n      .filter(\r\n        (redemption) =>\r\n          new Date(redemption.start_at).getTime() > Date.now() &&\r\n          !["cancelled", "canceled"].includes(redemption.status.toLowerCase()),\r\n      )\r\n      .sort(\r\n        (a, b) => new Date(a.start_at).getTime() - new Date(b.start_at).getTime(),\r\n      )[0] ?? null;\r\n  }, [bookingQuery.data?.bookings]);\r\n\r\n  const session = React.useMemo(() => {\r\n    if (!nextBooking) return null;\r\n    const minutes = Math.round(\r\n      (new Date(nextBooking.end_at).getTime() -\r\n        new Date(nextBooking.start_at).getTime()) /\r\n        60_000,\r\n    );\r\n    return {\r\n      startsAt: nextBooking.start_at,\r\n      durationMin: Number.isFinite(minutes) && minutes > 0 ? minutes : null,\r\n      label: sessionLabel(nextBooking.start_at),\r\n      meta: [\r\n        // The instructor is named for a student, whose session it is with him.\r\n        // Staff looking at their own classroom get the plain duration: "with\r\n        // Joshua" reads as nonsense when you are Joshua.\r\n        Number.isFinite(minutes) && minutes > 0\r\n          ? permissions.is_admin\r\n            ? `${minutes} minutes`\r\n            : `${minutes} minutes with Joshua`\r\n          : null,\r\n        untilLabel(nextBooking.start_at),\r\n      ]\r\n        .filter(Boolean)\r\n        .join(" · "),\r\n    };\r\n  }, [nextBooking, permissions.is_admin]);\r\n\r\n  // The booking link follows the plan the student is actually on: credits in\r\n  // hand go straight to the picker, a pay-per-session student gets the single\r\n  // link, a spent package gets offered back as a renewal.\r\n  const booking = React.useMemo(\r\n    () =>\r\n      resolveStudentBookingCta({\r\n        pass: bookingQuery.data?.pass ?? null,\r\n        offers: bookingQuery.data?.offers ?? This is the student's own profile row and\n+  // deliberately selects only the test date needed for the top panel.\n+  const testDateQuery = useQuery({\n+    queryKey: [\"classroom-home:test-date\", user?.id],\n+    enabled: !!user && !permissions.is_admin,\n+    staleTime: 5 * 60_000,\n+    queryFn: async () => {\n+      const { data, error } = await supabase\n+        .from(\"profiles\")\n+        .select(\"test_date\")\n+        .eq(\"id\", user!.id)\n+        .single();\n+      if (error) throw error;\n+      return data.test_date;\n+    },\n+  });\n@@\n   const model: ClassroomHomeModel = {\n@@\n     dateline: datelineFor(now),\n     subtitle: subtitleFor(dueTodayCount, workRows.length, unread),\n+    daysToTest: (() => {\n+      const testDate = testDateQuery.data;\n+      if (!testDate) return null;\n+      const parsed = new Date(`${testDate.slice(0, 10)}T00:00:00`);\n+      const delta = Math.round((parsed.getTime() - new Date(`${today}T00:00:00`).getTime()) / 86_400_000);\n+      return Number.isFinite(delta) && delta > 0 ? delta : null;\n+    })(),\n*** End Patch";

(\r\n        <SectionBriefing\r\n          briefing={briefing}\r\n          onStart={() => onOpen(briefing.href)}\r\n          onDismiss={() => setBriefing(null)}\r\n        />\r\n      ) : null}\r\n\r\n      <section className="classroom-home-top" aria-label="Today">\r\n        <header className="classroom-home-masthead">\r\n          <div className="classroom-home-masthead-text">\r\n            <span className="classroom-home-eyebrow"><i className="classroom-home-eyebrow-dot" aria-hidden="true" />{model.dateline}</span>\r\n            <h1 className="classroom-home-title">{model.greeting}{model.displayName ? <>, <em>{model.displayName}</em>.</> : "."}</h1>\r\n            <p className="classroom-home-subtitle">{model.subtitle}</p>\r\n            <div className="classroom-home-stat-row">\r\n              {/* LR questions this week is a named PR 2 slot; it intentionally renders nothing in PR 1. */}\r\n              {testDate !== null ? <div className="classroom-home-stat classroom-home-test-days" aria-label={`${testDate} ${testDateLabel}`}><strong>{testDate}</strong><span>{testDateLabel}</span></div> : null}\r\n            </div>\r\n          </div>\r\n          <div className="classroom-home-top-tools">\r\n            <div className="classroom-home-finderbar"><QuestionFinder ref={finderRef} returnTo="/classroom" /></div>\r\n            {continueRow ? <button type="button" className="classroom-home-action classroom-home-action-continue" aria-label="Continue assigned work" onClick={() => handleOpenRow(continueRow)}>\r\n              <span className="classroom-home-continue-copy"><span className="classroom-home-action-top">{continueRow.overdue ? (\r\n            <section className="classroom-home-hero" aria-label="Pick up where you left off">\r\n              <i className="classroom-home-sheet classroom-home-sheet-back" aria-hidden="true" />\r\n              <i className="classroom-home-sheet classroom-home-sheet-front" aria-hidden="true" />\r\n              <div className="classroom-home-hero-panel">\r\n                <i className="classroom-home-hero-glow" aria-hidden="true" />\r\n                <div className="classroom-home-hero-body">\r\n                  <div className="classroom-home-hero-main">\r\n                    <span className="classroom-home-hero-eyebrow">\r\n                      <i aria-hidden="true" />\r\n                      {hero.eyebrow}\r\n                    </span>\r\n                    <div className="classroom-home-hero-heading">\r\n                      <h2>{hero.row.title}</h2>\r\n                      <span className="classroom-home-hero-meta">\r\n                        <span className="classroom-home-tag">{hero.kindLabel}</span>\r\n                        {hero.meta}\r\n                      </span>\r\n                    </div>\r\n                    {hero.prose ? (\r\n                      <p className="classroom-home-hero-prose">{hero.prose}</p>\r\n                    ) : null}\r\n                    <div className="classroom-home-hero-actions">\r\n                      <button\r\n                        type="button"\r\n                        className="classroom-home-cta"\r\n                        onClick={() => handleOpenRow(hero.row)}\r\n                      >\r\n                        {hero.primaryLabel}\r\n                        <ArrowRight size={15} strokeWidth={2.2} />\r\n                      </button>\r\n                      {hero.row.canComplete && onMarkDone ? (\r\n                        <button\r\n                          type="button"\r\n                          className="classroom-home-ghost"\r\n                          disabled={completingKey === hero.row.key}\r\n                          onClick={() => onMarkDone(hero.row.key)}\r\n                        >\r\n                          {completingKey === hero.row.key ? (\r\n                    <aside className="classroom-home-progress" aria-label="Progress">\r\n                      <span className="classroom-home-progress-label">PROGRESS</span>\r\n                      <span className="classroom-home-progress-count">\r\n                        <b>{hero.progress.completed ?? hero.progress.total}</b>\r\n                        <span>\r\n                          {hero.progress.completed === null\r\n                            ? hero.progress.unit\r\n                            : `of ${hero.progress.total} ${hero.progress.unit}`}\r\n                        </span>\r\n                      </span>\r\n                      {hero.progress.completed !== null ? "classroom-home-tick classroom-home-tick-done"\r\n                                  : "classroom-home-tick"\r\n                              }\r\n                            />\r\n                          ))}\r\n                        </div>\r\n                      ) : null}\r\n                      {hero.progress.estimateMinutes ? (\r\n                  // Keyed by the message: clearing one animates the row off\r\n                  // the page, and the next message has to arrive in a fresh\r\n                  // row rather than inheriting that swiped-away transform.\r\n                  <SwipeableDeleteRow\r\n                    key={model.message.id}\r\n                    label={`message from ${model.message.from}`}\r\n                    rounded="rounded-xl"\r\n                    actionLabel="Clear"\r\n                    actionGerund="clearing"\r\n                    onDelete={() => onClearMessage(model.message!.id)}\r\n                  >\r\n                    <MessagePreviewButton message={model.message} onOpen={handleOpen} />\r\n                  </SwipeableDeleteRow>\r\n                ) : (\r\n                  <MessagePreviewButton message={model.message} onOpen={handleOpen} />\r\n                )}\r\n              </div>\r\n            ) : (\r\n              <p className="classroom-home-empty classroom-home-empty-boxed">\r\n                No messages yet.\r\n              </p>\r\n            )}\r\n          </section>\r\n          ) : null}\r\n\r\n          <section className="classroom-home-card classroom-home-session classroom-home-next-session" aria-label="Next session">\r\n            <span className="classroom-home-session-icon" aria-hidden="true">\r\n              <CalendarClock size={17} strokeWidth={1.8} />\r\n            </span>\r\n            <span className="classroom-home-session-text">\r\n              <span className="classroom-home-card-label classroom-home-card-label-muted">\r\n                NEXT SESSION\r\n              </span>\r\n              <span className="classroom-home-session-value">{model.session ? <><strong>{new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", { hour: "numeric", minute: "2-digit" }).format(new Date(model.session.startsAt)).replace(/\\s?(AM|PM)$/i, "")}</strong><small>{new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", { hour: "numeric" }).format(new Date(model.session.startsAt)).match(/(AM|PM)$/i)?.[0]?.toLowerCase() ?? ""}</small></> : "Nothing booked"}</span>\r\n              {model.session ? <span className="classroom-home-session-date">{new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", { weekday: "long", month: "long", day: "numeric" }).format(new Date(model.session.startsAt))} · {model.session.durationMin ? `${model.session.durationMin} minutes` : "Session"}</span> : <span className="classroom-home-session-date">Choose a time that works for you.</span>}\r\n              {model.session?.meta ? (\r\n                <span className="classroom-home-session-meta">{model.session.meta}</span>\r\n              ) : null}\r\n              {model.booking?.detail ? (\r\n                <span className="classroom-home-session-meta">{model.booking.detail}</span>\r\n              ) : null}\r\n            </span>\r\n            <span className="classroom-home-session-actions">\r\n              {/* No Reschedule pill: neither student-booking nor public-booking\r\n                  exposes a reschedule action. The booking pill itself follows\r\n                  the student\'s plan — credits go to the in-app picker, a\r\n                  pay-per-session student gets their own link. */}\r\n              <Pill\r\n                emphasis\r\n                onClick={() => {\r\n                  const target = model.booking?.href ?? "/booking";\r\n                  if (model.booking?.external) window.open(target, "_blank", "noopener,noreferrer");\r\n                  else handleOpen(target);\r\n                }}\r\n              >\r\n                {model.booking?.label ?? (model.session ? "Book another" : "Book one")}\r\n              </Pill>\r\n            </span>\r\n          </section>\r\n          {model.access.activities ? (\r\n            <section\r\n              className="classroom-home-card classroom-home-session classroom-home-tile classroom-home-rrc-tile"\r\n              aria-label="Read, Recall, Colour"\r\n            >\r\n              <span className="classroom-home-session-icon" aria-hidden="true">\r\n                <ArrowRight size={17} strokeWidth={1.8} />\r\n              </span>\r\n              <span className="classroom-home-session-text">\r\n                <span className="classroom-home-card-label classroom-home-card-label-muted">\r\n                  READ, RECALL, COLOUR\r\n                </span>\r\n                <span className="classroom-home-session-value">Start a section</span>\r\n                <span className="classroom-home-session-meta">\r\n                  Read a full section aloud, then get it graded.\r\n                </span>\r\n              </span>\r\n              <span className="classroom-home-session-actions">\r\n                <Pill emphasis onClick={() => handleOpen("/activities/sections")}>\r\n                  Choose a section\r\n                </Pill>\r\n              </span>\r\n            </section>\r\n          ) : null}\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n        </div>\r\n      </div>\r\n\r\n      <div className="classroom-home-lower-row">\r\n        {model.access.messages && model.message ? null : null}\r\n      </div>\r\n\r\n      {/* ── this week ────────────────────────────────────────────────── */}\r\n      <section className="classroom-home-week" aria-label="Next seven days">\r\n        <header className="classroom-home-rule">\r\n          <span className="classroom-home-card-label">NEXT SEVEN DAYS</span>\r\n          <i className="classroom-home-rule-draw" aria-hidden="true" />\r\n          <span className="classroom-home-week-range">{model.weekLabel}</span>\r\n        </header>\r\n        <div className="classroom-home-days">\r\n          {model.week.map((day) => {\r\n            const tone = day.isToday\r\n              ? " classroom-home-day-today"\r\n              : day.label\r\n                ? " classroom-home-day-item"\r\n                : day.isWeekend\r\n                  ? " classroom-home-day-rest"\r\n                  : " classroom-home-day-open";\r\n            const body = (\r\n              <>\r\n                <span className="classroom-home-day-head">\r\n                  <span className="classroom-home-day-abbr">{day.abbr}</span>\r\n                  <span className="classroom-home-day-num">{day.dayNum}</span>\r\n                </span>\r\n                <span className="classroom-home-day-label">\r\n                  {day.label ? (\r\n                    <>\r\n                      <i aria-hidden="true" />\r\n                      {day.label}\r\n                    </>\r\n                  ) : day.isWeekend ? (\r\n                    "Rest"\r\n                  ) : (\r\n                    "Open"\r\n                  )}\r\n                </span>\r\n              </>\r\n            );\r\n\r\n            // The band still reads without schedule access; it just stops being\r\n            // a control, rather than becoming a button onto a locked route.\r\n            return model.access.schedule ? (\r\n              <button\r\n                key={day.iso}\r\n                type="button"\r\n                className={`classroom-home-day${tone}`}\r\n                onClick={() => handleOpen("/schedule")}\r\n              >\r\n                {body}\r\n              </button>\r\n            ) : (\r\n              <div key={day.iso} className={`classroom-home-day${tone}`}>\r\n                {body}\r\n              </div>\r\n            );\r\n          })}\r\n        </div>\r\n      </section>\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport default ClassroomHomeView;\r\n'}]

It uses SendInput, UI Automation, and Windows.Graphics.Capture screenshots that work even when windows are occluded.\n\nIf this plugin is available, read this entire `SKILL.md` once before Windows automation work, before saying Computer Use is unavailable, and before falling back to other Windows automation.\n\nStart with the directions in the Initialize section below. Read these bundled Markdown files relative to this `SKILL.md` when you need information about the specific topic they cover:\n\n- `../../docs/guidance.md`: core runtime behavior, target-window workflow, screenshot handling, and recovery guidance. You MUST read this before controlling Windows apps.\n- `../../docs/api.md`: full `sky` API reference. Read this when you need method signatures or object shapes.\n- `../../docs/confirmations.md`: you MUST read this before deciding whether a Windows UI action needs confirmation\n\n## Initialize\r\n\r\nThe bundled `cua_node` `@oai/sky` package is the core entry point for Computer Use. Import it directly by package name from the JavaScript session. Do not spawn `codex-computer-use.exe`, search for the helper executable, or build a custom helper protocol client.\r\n\r\nRun this once per fresh `node_repl` JavaScript session:\r\n\r\n```js\r\nif (!globalThis.sky) {\r\n  const { sky } = await import("@oai/sky");\r\n  globalThis.sky = sky;\r\n}\r\n```\n\r\n'}]
